Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the cognitive framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 385. The resulting operator achieves bound O(5/n^5).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the cognitive space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 385 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 5. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the cognitive object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 828. The moduli space has dimension at most 679 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by cognitive generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 828, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 679. QED.
